### FILE swegemma/graph/embedding_utils.py
"""Embedding utilities for graph embedding lookup from local storage."""

from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
from typing import Any

import cachetools
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM = 256
DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE = '/cns/iz-d/home/research-graph/public/ttl=4y/python_repos/gh_kaggle_v12/repo_graphs@351'
FALLBACK_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE = '/cns/iz-d/home/research-graph-nonconf/public/ttl=4y/g4swe/gh_kaggle_v12/repo_graphs@351'
DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE = '/cns/iz-d/home/research-graph/public/ttl=4y/python_repos/gh_kaggle_v11/final/github_kaggle_func_node_embeddings.sst@1392'
FALLBACK_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE = '/cns/iz-d/home/research-graph-nonconf/public/ttl=4y/g4swe/gh_kaggle_v12/final/github_kaggle_func_node_embeddings.sst@1392'

_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_ACTIVE_STATE: tuple[Any, ...] | None = None
_ACTIVE_REPO: str | None = None
_CACHE: cachetools.LRUCache = cachetools.LRUCache(maxsize=50000)
_REPO_CACHES: dict[str, dict[str, np.ndarray]] = {}


def is_local_path(path: str | None) -> bool:
    """Returns True if the path targets local storage rather than CNS or SSTable."""
    if not path:
        return False
    if any(path.endswith(ext) for ext in ('.json', '.npz')):
        return True
    if any(d in path for d in ('data/graphs', 'data/embeddings')):
        return True
    return os.path.exists(path)


def repo_to_file_path(base_dir: str, repo_name: str | bytes, ext: str) -> str:
    """Converts a repository name (e.g. 'owner/repo') into a local filesystem path."""
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )
    if not ext.startswith('.'):
        ext = '.' + ext
    return os.path.join(base_dir, f'{repo_str}{ext}')


def resolve_local_file_path(
    base_dir: str,
    repo_name: str | bytes,
    ext: str,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Resolves a local file path across nested and flat/snapshot filename conventions."""
    if base_dir.endswith(ext):
        return base_dir
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )
    if not ext.startswith('.'):
        ext = '.' + ext

    effective_commit = (
        None
        if (base_commit and repo_str.endswith(f'_{base_commit}'))
        else base_commit
    )
    repo_short = repo_str.rsplit('/', maxsplit=1)[-1]
    repo_slug = repo_str.replace('/', '_')

    candidates: list[str] = []
    if effective_commit:
        candidates.extend([
            os.path.join(base_dir, f'{repo_short}_{effective_commit}{ext}'),
            os.path.join(base_dir, f'{repo_slug}_{effective_commit}{ext}'),
            repo_to_file_path(base_dir, f'{repo_str}_{effective_commit}', ext),
        ])
    candidates.extend([
        repo_to_file_path(base_dir, repo_str, ext),
        os.path.join(base_dir, f'{repo_slug}{ext}'),
        os.path.join(base_dir, f'{repo_short}{ext}'),
    ])
    return next((p for p in candidates if os.path.exists(p)), candidates[0])


def clear_caches(repo_name: str | bytes | None = None) -> None:
    """Clears the in-memory embedding cache for a specific repo or all repos."""
    global _ACTIVE_STATE, _ACTIVE_REPO  # noqa: PLW0603
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else repo_name
    )
    with _LOCK:
        if repo_str is not None:
            if repo_str == _ACTIVE_REPO or (
                _ACTIVE_REPO and _ACTIVE_REPO.startswith(f'{repo_str}_')
            ):
                _CACHE.clear()
                _ACTIVE_STATE = None
                _ACTIVE_REPO = None
            for k in list(_REPO_CACHES.keys()):
                if k == repo_str or k.startswith(f'{repo_str}_'):
                    _REPO_CACHES.pop(k, None)
        else:
            _CACHE.clear()
            _ACTIVE_STATE = None
            _ACTIVE_REPO = None
            _REPO_CACHES.clear()


def save_embeddings_to_npz(
    repo_name: str | bytes,
    node_embeddings: dict[str | bytes, Any],
    output_dir: str = 'data/embeddings',
) -> str:
    """Saves a dictionary of node embeddings to a compressed NumPy (.npz) file.

    Args:
        repo_name: The name of the repository (e.g. '0913ktg/vits_korean_speaker').
        node_embeddings: Dictionary mapping node names/texts to float32 NumPy arrays.
        output_dir: Base directory where embeddings are locally saved.

    Returns:
        The full path to the written .npz file.
    """
    if not repo_name or node_embeddings is None:
        raise ValueError(
            'repo_name and node_embeddings cannot be empty/None when saving'
            ' embeddings.'
        )
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )
    file_path = repo_to_file_path(output_dir, repo_str, '.npz')
    dir_name = os.path.dirname(file_path)
    if dir_name and not os.path.exists(dir_name):
        os.makedirs(dir_name, exist_ok=True)

    target_dim = next(
        (
            len(v)
            for v in node_embeddings.values()
            if v is not None and len(v) > 0
        ),
        DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM,
    )

    cleaned_embeddings: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
    for k, v in node_embeddings.items():
        key_str = (
            k.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(k, bytes) else str(k)
        )
        if v is not None:
            cleaned_embeddings[key_str] = np.array(v, dtype=np.float32)
        else:
            cleaned_embeddings[key_str] = np.zeros(target_dim, dtype=np.float32)

    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    np.savez_compressed(buffer, **cleaned_embeddings)
    buffer.seek(0)

    with open(file_path, 'wb') as f:
        f.write(buffer.read())

    clear_caches(repo_str)
    logger.info(
        'Saved %d embeddings for repo %s to %s using NumPy v%s.',
        len(cleaned_embeddings),
        repo_str,
        file_path,
        np.__version__,
    )
    return file_path


def load_embeddings_from_npz(file_path: str) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Loads node embeddings from a compressed NumPy (.npz) file.

    Args:
        file_path: Path to the .npz archive.

    Returns:
        Dictionary mapping node names/texts to float32 NumPy arrays.
    """
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f'Embeddings file not found at: {file_path}')
    with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
        buffer = io.BytesIO(f.read())
    try:
        with np.load(buffer, allow_pickle=False) as data:
            result = {
                str(k): np.array(data[k], dtype=np.float32) for k in data.files
            }
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f'Failed to load pure float32 numerical arrays from {file_path}'
            ' (allow_pickle=False). Archive may contain unsupported object types or'
            f' corrupted headers: {exc}'
        ) from exc
    return result


def embed(
    node_content: str | bytes | None,
    repo_name: str | bytes | None,
    graphs_sstable_path: str = DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> np.ndarray | None:
    """Returns the numerical single-precision embedding vector for the given code content/node.

    Utilizes an in-memory bounded LRU cache per graph. Ensures only the active
    graph's data is loaded into memory at any given time.

    Args:
        node_content: The node name (e.g. 'commons.shift_1d') or code content string.
        repo_name: The repository name (e.g. '0913ktg/vits_korean_speaker').
        graphs_sstable_path: Path to the repo_graphs SSTable or local graphs path.
        embeddings_sstable_path: Path to the function embeddings SSTable or local NPZ.
        use_local_storage: Whether to read embeddings from local files.
        embeddings_dir: Optional local embeddings directory override.
        graph_dir: Optional local graphs directory override.
        base_commit: Optional base commit hash for snapshot-specific lookup.

    Returns:
        A numpy array of float32, or None if not found.
    """
    if not node_content or not repo_name:
        return None

    if isinstance(node_content, bytes):
        node_content = node_content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
    if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
        repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    if embeddings_dir is not None:
        embeddings_sstable_path = embeddings_dir
        use_local_storage = True
    if graph_dir is not None:
        graphs_sstable_path = graph_dir
        use_local_storage = True

    if node_content.startswith(f'{repo_name};'):
        node_content = node_content[len(f'{repo_name};') :]

    effective_commit = (
        None
        if (base_commit and repo_name.endswith(f'_{base_commit}'))
        else base_commit
    )
    if effective_commit and node_content.startswith(
        f'{repo_name}_{effective_commit};'
    ):
        node_content = node_content[len(f'{repo_name}_{effective_commit};') :]

    effective_repo = (
        f'{repo_name}_{effective_commit}' if effective_commit else repo_name
    )

    global _ACTIVE_STATE, _ACTIVE_REPO  # noqa: PLW0603

    with _LOCK:
        current_state = (
            effective_repo,
            graphs_sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path,
            'local',
        )

        if current_state != _ACTIVE_STATE:
            _CACHE.clear()
            _ACTIVE_STATE = None
            _ACTIVE_REPO = None

            if embeddings_sstable_path.endswith('.npz'):
                local_path = embeddings_sstable_path
            else:
                base_emb_dir = (
                    embeddings_sstable_path
                    if (
                        (
                            use_local_storage
                            and embeddings_sstable_path
                            not in (
                                DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
                                FALLBACK_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
                            )
                        )
                        or is_local_path(embeddings_sstable_path)
                    )
                    else 'data/embeddings'
                )
                local_path = resolve_local_file_path(
                    base_emb_dir, repo_name, '.npz', base_commit=effective_commit
                )

            if not os.path.exists(local_path):
                logger.warning('Local embeddings NPZ file not found at %s', local_path)
                _ACTIVE_STATE = current_state
                _ACTIVE_REPO = effective_repo
                _REPO_CACHES[effective_repo] = {}
            else:
                try:
                    loaded_embs = load_embeddings_from_npz(local_path)
                    repo_cache: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
                    for k, v in loaded_embs.items():
                        _CACHE[k] = v
                        repo_cache[k] = v
                        if ';' in k:
                            bare_k = k.split(';', 1)[1]
                            if bare_k not in _CACHE:
                                _CACHE[bare_k] = v
                                repo_cache[bare_k] = v

                    # Also index node texts from local graph JSON if available (matching CNS mode)
                    base_graph_dir = (
                        graphs_sstable_path
                        if (
                            (
                                use_local_storage
                                and graphs_sstable_path
                                not in (
                                    DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
                                    FALLBACK_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
                                )
                            )
                            or is_local_path(graphs_sstable_path)
                        )
                        else os.path.join(
                            os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(local_path)), 'graphs'
                        )
                    )
                    local_graph_path = resolve_local_file_path(
                        base_graph_dir,
                        repo_name,
                        '.json',
                        base_commit=effective_commit,
                    )
                    if os.path.exists(local_graph_path):
                        try:
                            with open(local_graph_path, encoding='utf-8') as gf:
                                gdata = json.load(gf)
                            for n_obj in gdata.get('nodes', []):
                                if isinstance(n_obj, dict):
                                    nid = str(
                                        n_obj.get('name') or n_obj.get('id') or ''
                                    )
                                    ntext = n_obj.get('text') or n_obj.get('code')
                                    if (
                                        nid
                                        and ntext
                                        and isinstance(ntext, str)
                                        and nid in repo_cache
                                    ):
                                        _CACHE[ntext] = repo_cache[nid]
                                        repo_cache[ntext] = repo_cache[nid]
                        except Exception:
                            pass

                    _ACTIVE_STATE = current_state
                    _ACTIVE_REPO = effective_repo
                    _REPO_CACHES[effective_repo] = repo_cache
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(
                        'Failed to load local embeddings from %s: %s',
                        local_path,
                        e,
                        exc_info=True,
                    )
                    _ACTIVE_STATE = current_state
                    _ACTIVE_REPO = effective_repo
                    _REPO_CACHES[effective_repo] = {}

        if node_content in _CACHE:
            return _CACHE.get(node_content)

        repo_cache = _REPO_CACHES.get(effective_repo, {})
        if repo_cache and not any(c.isspace() for c in node_content):
            bare_q = (
                node_content.split(';', 1)[1]
                if ';' in node_content
                else node_content
            )
            symbol_keys = [
                k for k in repo_cache if not any(c.isspace() for c in k)
            ]
            suffix_matches = [
                k
                for k in symbol_keys
                if k.endswith(f'.{bare_q}')
                or k.endswith(f';{bare_q}')
                or k.endswith(f'/{bare_q}')
            ]
            if suffix_matches:
                best_k = min(
                    suffix_matches,
                    key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x),
                )
                emb = repo_cache.get(best_k)
                _CACHE[node_content] = emb
                return emb

            lower_q = bare_q.lower()
            ci_matches = [
                k
                for k in symbol_keys
                if k.lower() == lower_q
                or k.lower().endswith(f'.{lower_q}')
                or k.lower().endswith(f';{lower_q}')
                or k.lower().endswith(f'/{lower_q}')
            ]
            if ci_matches:
                best_k = min(
                    ci_matches,
                    key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x),
                )
                emb = repo_cache.get(best_k)
                _CACHE[node_content] = emb
                return emb

        _CACHE[node_content] = None
        return None


### FILE swegemma/graph/graph_utils.py
"""Graph utilities for constructing and inspecting repository graphs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import os
import threading
from typing import Any

import cachetools
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np

from swegemma.graph import embedding_utils

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE
FALLBACK_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE = embedding_utils.FALLBACK_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE

_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_SSTABLE_CACHE: dict[str, Any] = {}
_GRAPH_CACHE: cachetools.LRUCache = cachetools.LRUCache(maxsize=100)


def clear_caches() -> None:
    """Clears the in-memory SSTable and graph caches."""
    with _LOCK:
        _SSTABLE_CACHE.clear()
        _GRAPH_CACHE.clear()
        logger.info('Graph utils caches cleared.')


def clear_cache_for_repo(repo_name: str | bytes) -> None:
    """Clears the in-memory graph cache for a specific repository."""
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )
    with _LOCK:
        keys_to_remove = [
            k
            for k in _GRAPH_CACHE
            if k[0] == repo_str or str(k[0]).startswith(f'{repo_str}_')
        ]
        for k in keys_to_remove:
            del _GRAPH_CACHE[k]
        logger.info('Graph cache cleared for repo: %s', repo_str)


class CustomMultiEdgeView(nx.classes.reportviews.OutMultiEdgeView):
    """Custom OutMultiEdgeView that supports 2-tuple indexing (u, v) for MultiDiGraph."""

    def __getitem__(self, e: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(e, tuple) and len(e) == 2:
            u, v = e
            return self._adjdict[u][v]
        return super().__getitem__(e)


class CustomMultiDiGraph(nx.MultiDiGraph):
    """Custom MultiDiGraph whose edges property returns CustomMultiEdgeView."""

    @property
    def edges(self) -> CustomMultiEdgeView:
        return CustomMultiEdgeView(self)


def _clean_for_json(obj: Any, include_embeddings: bool = False) -> Any:
    """Recursively cleans an object for JSON serialization, handling bytes and numpy types."""
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        cleaned = {}
        for k, v in obj.items():
            k_str = k.decode('utf-8') if isinstance(k, bytes) else str(k)
            if k_str == 'embedding' and not include_embeddings:
                continue
            if k_str == 'embedding' and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):
                cleaned[k_str] = v.tolist()
            else:
                cleaned[k_str] = _clean_for_json(v, include_embeddings)
        return cleaned
    if isinstance(obj, (set, frozenset)):
        return sorted(
            [_clean_for_json(item, include_embeddings) for item in obj],
            key=str,
        )
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [_clean_for_json(item, include_embeddings) for item in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, bytes):
        return obj.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):
        return obj.tolist()
    if isinstance(obj, np.integer):
        return int(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, np.floating):
        return float(obj)
    return obj


def save_graph_to_json(
    graph: nx.DiGraph | nx.MultiDiGraph,
    output_dir: str = 'data/graphs',
    include_embeddings: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """Saves a networkx graph topology to a local JSON file using node_link_data.

    By default, node 'embedding' vectors are excluded from the JSON to maintain
    clean separation of structure (.json) and embedding features (.npz).

    Args:
        graph: A networkx DiGraph or MultiDiGraph to serialize.
        output_dir: Output base directory or target .json file path.
        include_embeddings: Whether to include node embedding vectors in JSON.

    Returns:
        The path of the written JSON file.
    """
    repo_name = getattr(graph, 'graph', {}).get('repo_name')
    if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
        repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    if output_dir.endswith('.json'):
        file_path = output_dir
    elif repo_name:
        file_path = embedding_utils.repo_to_file_path(
            output_dir, str(repo_name), '.json'
        )
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            "Graph missing required 'repo_name' attribute in graph.graph for JSON"
            ' output.'
        )

    dir_name = os.path.dirname(file_path)
    if dir_name and not os.path.exists(dir_name):
        os.makedirs(dir_name, exist_ok=True)

    raw_data = nx.node_link_data(graph, edges='edges')
    clean_data = _clean_for_json(raw_data, include_embeddings=include_embeddings)

    with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(clean_data, f, indent=2)

    logger.info(
        'Saved graph for repo %s to JSON file %s',
        repo_name or 'unknown',
        file_path,
    )
    return file_path


def load_graph_from_json(file_path: str) -> CustomMultiDiGraph:
    """Loads a CustomMultiDiGraph from a JSON file serialized via node_link_data."""
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f'Graph JSON file not found at: {file_path}')

    with open(file_path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    edge_key = (
        'edges'
        if 'edges' in data
        else ('links' if 'links' in data else 'edges')
    )
    base_g = nx.node_link_graph(data, edges=edge_key)
    g = CustomMultiDiGraph(base_g)
    if hasattr(base_g, 'graph') and isinstance(base_g.graph, dict):
        g.graph.update(base_g.graph)

    for _n, attrs in g.nodes(data=True):
        if 'embedding' in attrs and attrs['embedding'] is not None:
            attrs['embedding'] = np.array(attrs['embedding'], dtype=np.float32)
    return g


def _discover_embedding_dim(
    node_names: list[str],
    repo_name: str,
    sstable_path: str,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str,
    use_local_storage: bool,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Discovers the embedding dimension by trying to embed nodes."""
    for name in node_names:
        try:
            emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                name,
                repo_name,
                graphs_sstable_path=sstable_path,
                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                base_commit=base_commit,
            )
            if emb is not None and len(emb) > 0:
                return len(emb)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return embedding_utils.DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM


def get_graph(
    repo_name: str | bytes,
    sstable_path: str = DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> CustomMultiDiGraph:
    """Returns a networkx MultiDiGraph for the given repository name."""
    if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
        repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    if graph_dir is not None:
        sstable_path = graph_dir
        use_local_storage = True
    if embeddings_dir is not None:
        embeddings_sstable_path = embeddings_dir
        use_local_storage = True

    effective_commit = (
        None
        if (base_commit and repo_name.endswith(f'_{base_commit}'))
        else base_commit
    )
    effective_repo = (
        f'{repo_name}_{effective_commit}' if effective_commit else repo_name
    )

    cache_key = (
        effective_repo,
        sstable_path,
        embeddings_sstable_path,
        use_local_storage,
    )
    with _LOCK:
        if cache_key in _GRAPH_CACHE:
            return _GRAPH_CACHE[cache_key]

    if sstable_path.endswith('.json'):
        local_path = sstable_path
    else:
        base_dir = (
            sstable_path
            if (
                (
                    use_local_storage
                    and sstable_path
                    not in (
                        DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
                        FALLBACK_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
                    )
                )
                or embedding_utils.is_local_path(sstable_path)
            )
            else 'data/graphs'
        )
        local_path = embedding_utils.resolve_local_file_path(
            base_dir, repo_name, '.json', base_commit=effective_commit
        )

    if not os.path.exists(local_path):
        raise KeyError(
            f'Repository {repo_name} not found in local files at {local_path}'
        )

    try:
        g = load_graph_from_json(local_path)
    except Exception as e:
        raise KeyError(
            f'Failed to load graph JSON for {repo_name} at {local_path}: {e}'
        ) from e

    g.graph['repo_name'] = repo_name

    # Discover target dimension for fallback
    target_dim = next(
        (
            len(attrs['embedding'])
            for _, attrs in g.nodes(data=True)
            if 'embedding' in attrs
            and attrs['embedding'] is not None
            and len(attrs['embedding']) > 0
        ),
        None,
    )
    if target_dim is None:
        node_names = [str(n) for n in g.nodes()]
        target_dim = _discover_embedding_dim(
            node_names,
            repo_name,
            sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path,
            use_local_storage=True,
            base_commit=effective_commit,
        )

    for n, attrs in g.nodes(data=True):
        if 'name' not in attrs:
            attrs['name'] = str(n)
        if 'text' not in attrs:
            attrs['text'] = ''
        if (
            'embedding' not in attrs
            or attrs['embedding'] is None
            or (
                isinstance(attrs['embedding'], np.ndarray)
                and len(attrs['embedding']) == 0
            )
        ):
            try:
                emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                    str(n),
                    repo_name,
                    graphs_sstable_path=sstable_path,
                    embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                    use_local_storage=True,
                    base_commit=effective_commit,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(
                    'embedding_utils.embed failed for local node %s in %s: %s',
                    n,
                    repo_name,
                    e,
                )
                emb = None
            if emb is None:
                emb = np.zeros(target_dim, dtype=np.float32)
            attrs['embedding'] = emb

    with _LOCK:
        _GRAPH_CACHE[cache_key] = g
    return g


def resolve_node_name(
    target_str: str | bytes,
    graph: Any,
) -> str | None:
    """Resolves a short or partially-qualified symbol name to a concrete node identifier in the graph.

    Matching precedence:
    1. Exact match (e.g. 'fastapi.applications.FastAPI')
    2. Suffix match on delimiter boundary (e.g. '.FastAPI', ';FastAPI', or '/FastAPI')
    3. Case-insensitive exact / boundary match
    4. Substring containment match (for single-token symbol queries)
    """
    if not graph:
        return None

    query = (
        target_str.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(target_str, bytes)
        else str(target_str)
    ).strip()
    if not query:
        return None

    bare_query = query.split(';', 1)[1] if ';' in query else query

    raw_nodes = (
        graph.nodes()
        if hasattr(graph, 'nodes') and callable(graph.nodes)
        else graph
    )
    node_list = [
        n.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(n, bytes) else str(n)
        for n in raw_nodes
    ]
    if not node_list:
        return None

    node_set = set(node_list)
    if query in node_set:
        return query
    if bare_query in node_set:
        return bare_query

    # 1. Exact suffix matching on delimiter boundary (., ;, /)
    candidates = [
        n
        for n in node_list
        if n.endswith(f'.{bare_query}')
        or n.endswith(f';{bare_query}')
        or n.endswith(f'/{bare_query}')
    ]
    if candidates:
        return min(
            candidates,
            key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x),
        )

    # 2. Case-insensitive exact or boundary match
    lower_query = bare_query.lower()
    lower_full = query.lower()
    ci_exact = [
        n for n in node_list if n.lower() == lower_full or n.lower() == lower_query
    ]
    if ci_exact:
        return min(ci_exact, key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x))

    ci_candidates = [
        n
        for n in node_list
        if n.lower().endswith(f'.{lower_query}')
        or n.lower().endswith(f';{lower_query}')
        or n.lower().endswith(f'/{lower_query}')
    ]
    if ci_candidates:
        return min(
            ci_candidates,
            key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x),
        )

    # 3. Substring containment match for symbol queries without whitespace
    if not any(c.isspace() for c in bare_query):
        sub_candidates = [
            n
            for n in node_list
            if bare_query in n or lower_query in n.lower()
        ]
        if sub_candidates:
            return min(
                sub_candidates,
                key=lambda x: (len(x.split('.')), len(x), x),
            )

    return None


def get_neighbor(
    node: str | bytes | list[str | bytes],
    graph: nx.DiGraph | nx.MultiDiGraph | None = None,
    max_neighbors: int = 50,
    edge_type: str | None = None,
    sstable_path: str = DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    repo_name: str | bytes | None = None,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Returns a list of neighboring node names or a dict of lists if node is a list."""
    if max_neighbors <= 0:
        raise ValueError('max_neighbors must be greater than 0.')

    if isinstance(node, list):
        return {
            n: get_neighbor(
                n,
                graph=graph,
                max_neighbors=max_neighbors,
                edge_type=edge_type,
                sstable_path=sstable_path,
                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                repo_name=repo_name,
                graph_dir=graph_dir,
                embeddings_dir=embeddings_dir,
                base_commit=base_commit,
            )
            for n in node
        }

    node_str = (
        node.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(node, bytes)
        else str(node)
    )
    if repo_name is not None and isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
        repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    graph_repo_name = getattr(graph, 'graph', {}).get('repo_name')
    if isinstance(graph_repo_name, bytes):
        graph_repo_name = graph_repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    eff_repo = repo_name or graph_repo_name
    target_node = node_str

    if ';' in node_str:
        parts = node_str.split(';', 1)
        if graph is None:
            eff_repo = eff_repo or parts[0]
            graph = get_graph(
                eff_repo,
                sstable_path=sstable_path,
                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                graph_dir=graph_dir,
                embeddings_dir=embeddings_dir,
                base_commit=base_commit,
            )
            target_node = parts[1]
        elif not graph.has_node(node_str) and graph.has_node(parts[1]):
            eff_repo = eff_repo or parts[0]
            target_node = parts[1]
        elif graph.has_node(node_str):
            target_node = node_str
            eff_repo = eff_repo or parts[0]
        else:
            eff_repo = eff_repo or parts[0]
            target_node = parts[1]
    elif graph is None:
        if eff_repo:
            graph = get_graph(
                eff_repo,
                sstable_path=sstable_path,
                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                graph_dir=graph_dir,
                embeddings_dir=embeddings_dir,
                base_commit=base_commit,
            )
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                "If graph is None, node must be in 'repo_name;node_name' format."
            )

    if graph is None:
        raise ValueError('graph cannot be None at this point.')

    if not graph.has_node(target_node):
        resolved = resolve_node_name(target_node, graph)
        if resolved is None or not graph.has_node(resolved):
            return []
        target_node = resolved

    edge_type_lower = str(edge_type).lower() if edge_type is not None else None
    neighbors: list[str] = []
    for _u, v, data in graph.out_edges(target_node, data=True):
        rel = data.get('type') or data.get('relation')
        if edge_type_lower is None or (
            rel is not None and str(rel).lower() == edge_type_lower
        ):
            neighbor_name = (
                f'{eff_repo};{v}'
                if eff_repo and not str(v).startswith(f'{eff_repo};')
                else str(v)
            )
            if neighbor_name not in neighbors:
                neighbors.append(neighbor_name)

    for u, _v, data in graph.in_edges(target_node, data=True):
        rel = data.get('type') or data.get('relation')
        if edge_type_lower is None or (
            rel is not None and str(rel).lower() == edge_type_lower
        ):
            neighbor_name = (
                f'{eff_repo};{u}'
                if eff_repo and not str(u).startswith(f'{eff_repo};')
                else str(u)
            )
            if neighbor_name not in neighbors:
                neighbors.append(neighbor_name)

    neighbors.sort()
    return neighbors[:max_neighbors]


def get_induced_subgraph(
    graph: nx.DiGraph | nx.MultiDiGraph,
    subset_nodes: list[str | bytes] | None = None,
) -> nx.DiGraph | nx.MultiDiGraph:
    """Returns the induced subgraph for the given subset of nodes."""
    if not subset_nodes:
        raise ValueError('subset_nodes cannot be empty.')
    valid_nodes = []
    for raw_n in subset_nodes:
        n = (
            raw_n.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            if isinstance(raw_n, bytes)
            else str(raw_n)
        )
        if graph.has_node(n):
            valid_nodes.append(n)
        elif ';' in n and graph.has_node(n.split(';', 1)[1]):
            valid_nodes.append(n.split(';', 1)[1])
        else:
            resolved = resolve_node_name(n, graph)
            if resolved is not None and graph.has_node(resolved):
                valid_nodes.append(resolved)
    return graph.subgraph(valid_nodes).copy()


### FILE swegemma/graph/retrieval_utils.py
"""Retrieval utilities for finding similar code nodes in repository graphs using embeddings."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re
import threading
from typing import Any

import cachetools
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np

from swegemma.graph import embedding_utils, graph_utils

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_LOCK = threading.Lock()

# In-memory caches
# Maps (repo_name, str(node_name), k, str(node_content), graphs_sstable_path,
# embeddings_sstable_path, use_local_storage) -> list of similar node dicts
_SIMILARITY_CACHE: cachetools.LRUCache = cachetools.LRUCache(maxsize=10000)

# Maps (repo_name, graphs_sstable_path, embeddings_sstable_path, use_local_storage) -> dict
# mapping node_name -> list of top-K similar node dicts
_PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS: dict[
    tuple[Any, ...], dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]
] = {}

# Maps (repo_name, graphs_sstable_path, embeddings_sstable_path, use_local_storage) -> dict
# mapping node_name -> node text
_PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS: dict[tuple[Any, ...], dict[str, str]] = {}


def clear_caches(repo_name: str | bytes | None = None) -> None:
    """Clears the in-memory similarity and precomputed caches.

    Args:
        repo_name: If provided, clears only the caches associated with this repo.
            If None, clears all caches.
    """
    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else repo_name
    )
    with _LOCK:
        if repo_str is None:
            _SIMILARITY_CACHE.clear()
            _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS.clear()
            _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS.clear()
            graph_utils.clear_caches()
            embedding_utils.clear_caches()
            logger.info('All retrieval caches cleared.')
        else:
            g_keys = [
                k
                for k in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS
                if (isinstance(k, tuple) and (k[0] == repo_str or str(k[0]).startswith(f'{repo_str}_')))
                or k == repo_str
                or str(k).startswith(f'{repo_str}_')
            ]
            for k in g_keys:
                del _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[k]
            t_keys = [
                k
                for k in _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS
                if (isinstance(k, tuple) and (k[0] == repo_str or str(k[0]).startswith(f'{repo_str}_')))
                or k == repo_str
                or str(k).startswith(f'{repo_str}_')
            ]
            for k in t_keys:
                del _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS[k]
            keys_to_remove = [
                k
                for k in _SIMILARITY_CACHE
                if isinstance(k, tuple)
                and len(k) > 0
                and (k[0] == repo_str or str(k[0]).startswith(f'{repo_str}_'))
            ]
            for k in keys_to_remove:
                del _SIMILARITY_CACHE[k]
            graph_utils.clear_cache_for_repo(repo_str)
            embedding_utils.clear_caches(repo_str)
            logger.info('Retrieval caches cleared for repo: %s', repo_str)


def _finalize_embedding_matrix(
    node_names: list[str],
    raw_embeddings: list[np.ndarray | None],
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Converts a list of embedding arrays (or None) into a 2D float32 matrix."""
    if not node_names:
        return np.empty(
            (0, embedding_utils.DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM), dtype=np.float32
        )

    detected_dim = next(
        (
            len(e)
            for e in raw_embeddings
            if e is not None and len(e) > 0
        ),
        embedding_utils.DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM,
    )
    embeddings_list: list[np.ndarray] = []
    for emb in raw_embeddings:
        if emb is None or len(emb) == 0:
            embeddings_list.append(np.zeros(detected_dim, dtype=np.float32))
        else:
            arr = np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32).flatten()
            if arr.shape[0] != detected_dim:
                adjusted = np.zeros(detected_dim, dtype=np.float32)
                dim = min(arr.shape[0], detected_dim)
                adjusted[:dim] = arr[:dim]
                embeddings_list.append(adjusted)
            else:
                embeddings_list.append(arr)

    embeddings_array = np.array(embeddings_list, dtype=np.float32)
    if embeddings_array.ndim == 1:
        embeddings_array = embeddings_array.reshape(len(node_names), -1)
    return embeddings_array


def _extract_nodes_and_embeddings(
    graph: Any,
    repo_name: str | bytes,
    graphs_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], np.ndarray]:
    """Extracts node names, code texts, and single-precision embedding vectors from various graph representations.

    Args:
        graph: A GraphStruct, networkx.DiGraph, networkx.Graph, or dict representation of a graph.
        repo_name: The name of the repository.
        graphs_sstable_path: Path to the repo_graphs SSTable or local graphs path.
        embeddings_sstable_path: Path to the function embeddings SSTable or local NPZ.
        use_local_storage: Whether to read embeddings from local files.
        embeddings_dir: Optional local embeddings directory override.
        graph_dir: Optional local graphs directory override.
        base_commit: Optional base commit hash for snapshot-specific lookup.

    Returns:
        A tuple of (node_names, node_texts, embeddings_array).
    """
    if embeddings_dir is not None:
        embeddings_sstable_path = embeddings_dir
        use_local_storage = True
    if graph_dir is not None:
        graphs_sstable_path = graph_dir
        use_local_storage = True

    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )

    node_names: list[str] = []
    node_texts: list[str] = []
    raw_embeddings: list[np.ndarray | None] = []

    if graph is None:
        return (
            [],
            [],
            np.empty(
                (0, embedding_utils.DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_DIM), dtype=np.float32
            ),
        )

    # 1. networkx.DiGraph or networkx.Graph
    if isinstance(graph, (nx.DiGraph, nx.Graph)):
        for n, data in graph.nodes(data=True):
            name = data.get('name', str(n))
            if isinstance(name, bytes):
                name = name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            text = data.get('text', data.get('code', ''))
            if text is None:
                text = ''
            if isinstance(text, bytes):
                text = text.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            emb = data.get('embedding')

            if emb is None or (isinstance(emb, np.ndarray) and emb.size == 0):
                emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                    text or name,
                    repo_str,
                    graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                    embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                    use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                    base_commit=base_commit,
                )
                if emb is None and text:
                    emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                        name,
                        repo_str,
                        graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                        base_commit=base_commit,
                    )

            node_names.append(str(name))
            node_texts.append(str(text))
            raw_embeddings.append(
                np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32) if emb is not None else None
            )

    # 2. GraphStruct
    elif (
        hasattr(graph, 'nodes')
        and isinstance(graph.nodes, dict)
        and 'nodes' in graph.nodes
    ):
        nodes_feat = graph.nodes['nodes']
        raw_names = nodes_feat.get('name', [])
        raw_texts = nodes_feat.get('text', [])
        raw_embs = nodes_feat.get('embedding')

        node_names = [
            s.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(s, bytes) else str(s)
            for s in raw_names
        ]
        node_texts = [
            s.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(s, bytes) else str(s)
            for s in raw_texts
        ]
        while len(node_texts) < len(node_names):
            node_texts.append('')

        if raw_embs is not None and len(raw_embs) == len(node_names):
            for emb in raw_embs:
                raw_embeddings.append(np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32))
        else:
            for i, name in enumerate(node_names):
                text = node_texts[i]
                emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                    text or name,
                    repo_str,
                    graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                    embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                    use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                    base_commit=base_commit,
                )
                if emb is None and text:
                    emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                        name,
                        repo_str,
                        graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                        base_commit=base_commit,
                    )
                raw_embeddings.append(
                    np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32) if emb is not None else None
                )

    # 3. dict representation
    elif isinstance(graph, dict):
        nodes_dict = graph.get('nodes', graph)
        if isinstance(nodes_dict, dict):
            if 'name' in nodes_dict:
                raw_names = nodes_dict.get('name', [])
                raw_texts = nodes_dict.get('text', nodes_dict.get('code', []))
                if raw_texts is None:
                    raw_texts = []
                raw_embs = nodes_dict.get('embedding')

                node_names = [
                    s.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                    if isinstance(s, bytes)
                    else str(s)
                    for s in raw_names
                ]
                node_texts = [
                    s.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                    if isinstance(s, bytes)
                    else str(s)
                    for s in raw_texts
                ]
                while len(node_texts) < len(node_names):
                    node_texts.append('')

                if raw_embs is not None and len(raw_embs) == len(node_names):
                    for emb in raw_embs:
                        raw_embeddings.append(np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32))
                else:
                    for i, name in enumerate(node_names):
                        text = node_texts[i]
                        emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                            text or name,
                            repo_str,
                            graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                            embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                            use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                            base_commit=base_commit,
                        )
                        if emb is None and text:
                            emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                                name,
                                repo_str,
                                graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                                base_commit=base_commit,
                            )
                        raw_embeddings.append(
                            np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32)
                            if emb is not None
                            else None
                        )
            else:
                for n, data in nodes_dict.items():
                    if isinstance(data, dict):
                        name_val = data.get('name', str(n))
                        name = (
                            name_val.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                            if isinstance(name_val, bytes)
                            else str(name_val)
                        )
                        text_val = data.get('text', data.get('code', ''))
                        if text_val is None:
                            text_val = ''
                        text = (
                            text_val.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                            if isinstance(text_val, bytes)
                            else str(text_val)
                        )
                        emb = data.get('embedding')

                        if emb is None:
                            emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                                text or name,
                                repo_str,
                                graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                                base_commit=base_commit,
                            )
                            if emb is None and text:
                                emb = embedding_utils.embed(
                                    name,
                                    repo_str,
                                    graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                                    embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                                    use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                                    base_commit=base_commit,
                                )

                        node_names.append(name)
                        node_texts.append(text)
                        raw_embeddings.append(
                            np.array(emb, dtype=np.float32)
                            if emb is not None
                            else None
                        )

    embeddings_array = _finalize_embedding_matrix(node_names, raw_embeddings)
    return node_names, node_texts, embeddings_array


def precompute_top_k_similar_nodes(
    repo_name: str | bytes,
    k: int = 10,
    graphs_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    chunk_size: int = 1000,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    """Pre-computes and caches the top-K similar nodes for each node in a graph.

    Args:
        repo_name: The name of the repository graph to precompute.
        k: The number of nearest neighbors to compute for each node.
        graphs_sstable_path: Path to the repo_graphs SSTable or local graphs path.
        embeddings_sstable_path: Path to the function embeddings SSTable or local NPZ.
        use_local_storage: Whether to read embeddings from local files.
        chunk_size: Batch size for matrix multiplication.
        graph_dir: Optional local graphs directory override.
        embeddings_dir: Optional local embeddings directory override.
        base_commit: Optional base commit hash for snapshot-specific precomputation.

    Returns:
        A dictionary mapping each node_name to its top-K similar nodes in sorted order.
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError('k must be greater than 0.')

    if graph_dir is not None:
        graphs_sstable_path = graph_dir
        use_local_storage = True
    if embeddings_dir is not None:
        embeddings_sstable_path = embeddings_dir
        use_local_storage = True

    repo_str = (
        repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes)
        else str(repo_name)
    )
    effective_commit = (
        None
        if (base_commit and repo_str.endswith(f'_{base_commit}'))
        else base_commit
    )
    effective_repo = (
        f'{repo_str}_{effective_commit}' if effective_commit else repo_str
    )

    logger.info('Precomputing top-%d similar nodes for repo: %s', k, repo_str)
    graph = graph_utils.get_graph(
        repo_str,
        sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
        base_commit=effective_commit,
    )
    names, texts, embeddings = _extract_nodes_and_embeddings(
        graph,
        repo_str,
        graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
        base_commit=effective_commit,
    )

    cache_key = (
        effective_repo,
        graphs_sstable_path,
        embeddings_sstable_path,
        use_local_storage,
    )
    if not names:
        logger.warning('No nodes found in graph for repo %s.', repo_str)
        with _LOCK:
            _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[cache_key] = {}
            _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS[cache_key] = {}
        return {}

    # Normalize embeddings for cosine similarity
    norms = np.linalg.norm(embeddings, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    norms[norms == 0] = 1e-12
    normed_embeddings = embeddings / norms

    n_nodes = len(names)
    effective_k = min(k, n_nodes - 1) if n_nodes > 1 else 0

    repo_results: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    if effective_k > 0:
        batch_size = chunk_size
        for batch_start in range(0, n_nodes, batch_size):
            batch_end = min(batch_start + batch_size, n_nodes)
            batch_embeddings = normed_embeddings[batch_start:batch_end]
            sim_batch = np.dot(batch_embeddings, normed_embeddings.T)

            for local_i in range(batch_end - batch_start):
                global_i = batch_start + local_i
                node_name = names[global_i]
                sim_batch[local_i, global_i] = -np.inf

                valid_indices = [j for j in range(n_nodes) if j != global_i]
                sorted_indices = sorted(
                    valid_indices,
                    key=lambda j, s=sim_batch, loc_i=local_i: (
                        -float(s[loc_i, j]),
                        names[j],
                    ),
                )
                top_indices = sorted_indices[:effective_k]

                similar_nodes = [
                    {
                        'node_name': names[j],
                        'code': texts[j],
                        'similarity': float(sim_batch[local_i, j]),
                        'embedding': embeddings[j],
                    }
                    for j in top_indices
                ]
                repo_results[node_name] = similar_nodes
    else:
        for node_name in names:
            repo_results[node_name] = []

    with _LOCK:
        _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS[cache_key] = {
            names[i]: texts[i] for i in range(len(names))
        }
        _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[cache_key] = repo_results
    logger.info(
        'Successfully precomputed top-%d similar nodes for %d nodes in repo %s.',
        k,
        n_nodes,
        repo_str,
    )
    return repo_results


def get_similar_nodes(
    node: Any,
    repo_name: str | bytes | None = None,
    k: int = 10,
    use_memoization: bool = True,
    graph: Any | None = None,
    graphs_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_REPO_GRAPHS_SSTABLE,
    embeddings_sstable_path: str = embedding_utils.DEFAULT_FUNC_EMBEDDINGS_SSTABLE,
    use_local_storage: bool = False,
    *,
    graph_dir: str | None = None,
    embeddings_dir: str | None = None,
    base_commit: str | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Retrieves the top-K similar code nodes within the same repo based on graph embeddings.

    Args:
        node: The target node to find similarities for. Can be a dictionary
            containing 'node_name' (and optionally 'repo_name', 'code', 'text'), a
            string or bytes representing 'node_name' or 'repo_name;node_name', or an
            object with those attributes.
        repo_name: The name of the repository. Required if not provided within `node`.
        k: The number of nearest neighbors to retrieve (default K=10).
        use_memoization: Whether to use in-memory memoization/caching (default True).
        graph: Optional graph object to scope the retrieval to (e.g. an induced subgraph).
        graphs_sstable_path: Path to the repo_graphs SSTable or local graphs dir.
        embeddings_sstable_path: Path to the function embeddings SSTable or local embeddings dir.
        use_local_storage: Whether to read embeddings from local files.
        graph_dir: Optional local graphs directory override.
        embeddings_dir: Optional local embeddings directory override.
        base_commit: Optional base commit hash for snapshot-specific lookup.

    Returns:
        A list of dictionaries representing the most similar nodes, sorted by similarity.
        Each dictionary contains 'node_name', 'code', 'similarity', and 'embedding'.
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError('k must be greater than 0.')

    if graph_dir is not None:
        graphs_sstable_path = graph_dir
        use_local_storage = True
    if embeddings_dir is not None:
        embeddings_sstable_path = embeddings_dir
        use_local_storage = True

    if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
        repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    node_name = None
    node_content = None

    if isinstance(node, (str, bytes)):
        node_str = (
            node.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            if isinstance(node, bytes)
            else node
        )
        if ';' in node_str:
            parts = node_str.split(';', 1)
            repo_name = repo_name or parts[0]
            node_name = parts[1]
        else:
            node_name = node_str
    elif isinstance(node, dict):
        node_name = node.get('node_name') or node.get('name')
        if isinstance(node_name, bytes):
            node_name = node_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        repo_name = repo_name or node.get('repo_name')
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
            repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        node_content = node.get('code') or node.get('text')
        if isinstance(node_content, bytes):
            node_content = node_content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
    elif (
        hasattr(node, 'node_name')
        or hasattr(node, 'code')
        or hasattr(node, 'text')
    ):
        node_name = getattr(node, 'node_name', None)
        if isinstance(node_name, bytes):
            node_name = node_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        repo_name = repo_name or getattr(node, 'repo_name', None)
        if isinstance(repo_name, bytes):
            repo_name = repo_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
        node_content = getattr(node, 'code', None) or getattr(
            node, 'text', None
        )
        if isinstance(node_content, bytes):
            node_content = node_content.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

    if node_name and ';' in node_name:
        parts = node_name.split(';', 1)
        repo_name = repo_name or parts[0]
        node_name = parts[1]

    if not repo_name:
        raise ValueError(
            'repo_name must be provided either as an argument or within the node'
            ' object.'
        )

    if not node_name and not node_content:
        raise ValueError(
            'Either node_name or code content must be provided to find similar'
            ' nodes.'
        )

    effective_commit = (
        None
        if (base_commit and str(repo_name).endswith(f'_{base_commit}'))
        else base_commit
    )
    effective_repo = (
        f'{repo_name}_{effective_commit}' if effective_commit else str(repo_name)
    )

    is_custom_graph = graph is not None
    names = None
    texts = None
    embeddings = None

    # 1. Check precomputed graph cache
    if not is_custom_graph and use_memoization:
        precompute_key = (
            effective_repo,
            graphs_sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path,
            use_local_storage,
        )
        with _LOCK:
            if (
                precompute_key in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS
                and node_name is not None
                and node_name not in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[precompute_key]
                and not node_content
                and not any(c.isspace() for c in str(node_name))
            ):
                resolved_pre = graph_utils.resolve_node_name(
                    node_name, _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[precompute_key].keys()
                )
                if resolved_pre is not None:
                    node_name = resolved_pre
            has_precomputed = (
                precompute_key in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS
                and node_name is not None
                and node_name in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[precompute_key]
            )
            cached_list = (
                _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[precompute_key].get(node_name, [])
                if has_precomputed and node_name is not None
                else []
            )
            total_precomputed = (
                len(_PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS[precompute_key])
                if precompute_key in _PRECOMPUTED_GRAPHS
                else 0
            )
            has_cached_text = (
                precompute_key in _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS
                and node_name is not None
                and node_name in _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS[precompute_key]
            )
            cached_text = (
                _PRECOMPUTED_TEXTS[precompute_key].get(node_name)
                if has_cached_text and node_name is not None
                else None
            )

        if has_precomputed:
            is_modified = False
            if node_content:
                if has_cached_text:
                    if node_content != cached_text:
                        is_modified = True
                else:
                    temp_graph = graph_utils.get_graph(
                        repo_name,
                        sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                        base_commit=effective_commit,
                    )
                    names, texts, embeddings = _extract_nodes_and_embeddings(
                        temp_graph,
                        repo_name,
                        graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                        embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                        use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                        base_commit=effective_commit,
                    )
                    if node_name and node_name in names:
                        target_index = names.index(node_name)
                        if node_content != texts[target_index]:
                            is_modified = True

            if not is_modified:
                logger.info(
                    'Retrieving similar nodes for %s from precomputed cache.',
                    node_name,
                )
                if (
                    len(cached_list) >= k
                    or len(cached_list) == total_precomputed - 1
                ):
                    return cached_list[:k]
            else:
                logger.info(
                    'Node %s exists in precomputed cache but content was modified.'
                    ' Bypassing precomputed cache.',
                    node_name,
                )

    # 2. Check memoization cache
    cache_key = (
        effective_repo,
        str(node_name),
        k,
        str(node_content),
        graphs_sstable_path,
        embeddings_sstable_path,
        use_local_storage,
    )
    if not is_custom_graph and use_memoization:
        with _LOCK:
            if cache_key in _SIMILARITY_CACHE:
                logger.info(
                    'Retrieving similar nodes for %s from memoization cache.',
                    node_name,
                )
                return _SIMILARITY_CACHE[cache_key]

    # 3. Retrieve graph from graph_utils and extract nodes/embeddings if not already retrieved
    if graph is None:
        graph = graph_utils.get_graph(
            repo_name,
            sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
            use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
            base_commit=effective_commit,
        )

    if names is None or texts is None or embeddings is None:
        names, texts, embeddings = _extract_nodes_and_embeddings(
            graph,
            repo_name,
            graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
            use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
            base_commit=effective_commit,
        )

    if not names:
        logger.warning(
            'Graph for repo %s is empty or has no nodes. Returning empty similarity'
            ' list.',
            repo_name,
        )
        return []

    # 4. Obtain target embedding
    target_embedding = None
    target_index = -1

    if (
        node_name
        and node_name not in names
        and not node_content
        and not any(c.isspace() for c in str(node_name))
    ):
        resolved_name = graph_utils.resolve_node_name(node_name, names)
        if resolved_name is not None and resolved_name in names:
            node_name = resolved_name

    if node_name and node_name in names:
        target_index = names.index(node_name)
        existing_text = texts[target_index]
        if node_content and node_content != existing_text:
            logger.info(
                'Node %s exists but content was edited. Calling'
                ' embedding_utils.embed.',
                node_name,
            )
            target_embedding = embedding_utils.embed(
                node_content,
                repo_name,
                graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
                embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
                use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
                base_commit=effective_commit,
            )
            if target_embedding is not None:
                target_embedding = np.array(target_embedding, dtype=np.float32)
        else:
            target_embedding = embeddings[target_index]
    elif node_content or node_name:
        query_text = node_content or node_name
        logger.info(
            'Node %s not in graph or new content provided. Calling'
            ' embedding_utils.embed.',
            node_name,
        )
        target_embedding = embedding_utils.embed(
            query_text,
            repo_name,
            graphs_sstable_path=graphs_sstable_path,
            embeddings_sstable_path=embeddings_sstable_path,
            use_local_storage=use_local_storage,
            base_commit=effective_commit,
        )
        if target_embedding is not None:
            target_embedding = np.array(target_embedding, dtype=np.float32)

    if target_embedding is None:
        raw_q = str(node_content or node_name or '').strip().lower()
        tokens = [
            tok for tok in re.findall(r'[a-z0-9_]+', raw_q) if len(tok) >= 2
        ]
        if tokens and not node_content:
            max_possible = float(len(tokens)) * 4.0 + 0.5
            lexical_scores: list[tuple[float, int]] = []
            for idx, n_str in enumerate(names):
                n_low = n_str.lower()
                t_low = texts[idx].lower() if idx < len(texts) else ''
                score = 0.0
                for tok in tokens:
                    if tok in n_low:
                        score += 3.0
                    if tok in t_low:
                        score += 1.0
                if score > 0:
                    if (
                        not n_low.startswith(('test.', 'tests.'))
                        and '.test_' not in n_low
                    ):
                        score += 0.5
                    lexical_scores.append((score, idx))
            if lexical_scores:
                lexical_scores.sort(key=lambda item: (-item[0], names[item[1]]))
                top_lexical = lexical_scores[: min(k, len(lexical_scores))]
                results = [
                    {
                        'node_name': names[idx],
                        'code': texts[idx],
                        'similarity': round(min(1.0, sc / max_possible), 4),
                        'embedding': embeddings[idx],
                    }
                    for sc, idx in top_lexical
                ]
                if not is_custom_graph and use_memoization:
                    with _LOCK:
                        _SIMILARITY_CACHE[cache_key] = results
                return results

        logger.warning(
            'Could not obtain embedding for node %s in repo %s.',
            node_name,
            repo_name,
        )
        return []

    # 5. Compute cosine similarity
    target_norm = np.linalg.norm(target_embedding)
    if target_norm == 0:
        target_norm = 1e-12
    normed_target = target_embedding / target_norm

    norms = np.linalg.norm(embeddings, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    norms[norms == 0] = 1e-12
    normed_embeddings = embeddings / norms

    sims = np.dot(normed_embeddings, normed_target.T).flatten()

    if target_index != -1:
        sims[target_index] = -np.inf

    n_nodes = len(names)
    effective_k = min(k, n_nodes - 1 if target_index != -1 else n_nodes)
    if effective_k <= 0:
        return []

    valid_indices = [j for j in range(n_nodes) if j != target_index]
    sorted_indices = sorted(
        valid_indices, key=lambda j: (-float(sims[j]), names[j])
    )
    top_indices = sorted_indices[:effective_k]

    results = [
        {
            'node_name': names[j],
            'code': texts[j],
            'similarity': float(sims[j]),
            'embedding': embeddings[j],
        }
        for j in top_indices
    ]

    if not is_custom_graph and use_memoization:
        with _LOCK:
            _SIMILARITY_CACHE[cache_key] = results

    return results

_SKILL_TOOL_NAMES = frozenset({'load_skill', 'load_skill_resource', 'run_skill_script'})


def resolve_swegemma_adapter(
    base_model: Any,
    adapter_info: Any,
    model_registry: Any | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Resolve an adapter declaration to a configured LiteLlm model instance."""
    import copy

    adapter_name = getattr(adapter_info, 'name', str(adapter_info))
    if model_registry is not None and adapter_name in model_registry:
        return model_registry.get(adapter_name)

    if hasattr(base_model, 'model') and isinstance(base_model.model, str):
        target_model = f'openai/{adapter_name}'
        for prefix in ('openai/', 'hosted_vllm/', 'custom/'):
            if base_model.model.startswith(prefix):
                target_model = f'{prefix}{adapter_name}'
                break
        if hasattr(base_model, 'model_copy'):
            return base_model.model_copy(update={'model': target_model})
        cloned = copy.copy(base_model)
        cloned.model = target_model
        return cloned

    if isinstance(base_model, str):
        for prefix in ('openai/', 'hosted_vllm/', 'custom/'):
            if base_model.startswith(prefix):
                return f'{prefix}{adapter_name}'
        return f'openai/{adapter_name}'

    return base_model


def _sanitize_skill_tool_args(
    tool_name: str,
    tool_args: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Coerce single-element list strings and validate skill tool string arguments."""
    if tool_name not in _SKILL_TOOL_NAMES or not isinstance(tool_args, dict):
        return None

    str_keys = (
        ('skill_name',)
        if tool_name == 'load_skill'
        else ('skill_name', 'file_path')
    )
    errors: list[str] = []
    for key in str_keys:
        if key not in tool_args:
            continue
        val = tool_args[key]
        if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) == 1 and isinstance(val[0], str):
            tool_args[key] = val[0]
            val = val[0]
        if val is not None and not isinstance(val, str):
            errors.append(
                f"Argument '{key}' must be a string, got {type(val).__name__}."
            )

    if errors:
        return {
            'status': 'error',
            'error': '\n'.join(errors),
            'error_code': 'INVALID_ARGUMENTS',
        }
    return None


def _wrap_skill_toolset_tools(agent: Any, _visited: set[int] | None = None) -> None:
    """Wrap SkillToolset tools on compiled agents so direct run_async calls coerce list args."""
    if agent is None:
        return
    if _visited is None:
        _visited = set()
    obj_id = id(agent)
    if obj_id in _visited:
        return
    _visited.add(obj_id)

    raw_tools = getattr(agent, 'tools', None)
    if isinstance(raw_tools, (list, tuple)):
        for item in raw_tools:
            inner_agent = getattr(item, 'agent', None)
            if inner_agent is not None:
                _wrap_skill_toolset_tools(inner_agent, _visited)
            inner_tools = getattr(item, '_tools', None)
            if isinstance(inner_tools, (list, tuple)):
                for st in inner_tools:
                    st_name = getattr(st, 'name', '') or ''
                    if st_name in _SKILL_TOOL_NAMES and not getattr(
                        st, '_swegemma_wrapped', False
                    ):
                        orig_run_async = st.run_async

                        async def _wrapped_run_async(
                            *,
                            args: dict[str, Any],
                            tool_context: Any,
                            _orig: Any = orig_run_async,
                            _name: str = st_name,
                        ) -> Any:
                            err = _sanitize_skill_tool_args(_name, args)
                            if err is not None:
                                return err
                            try:
                                return await _orig(args=args, tool_context=tool_context)
                            except Exception as exc:
                                logger.warning(
                                    "Skill tool '%s' raised %s: %s",
                                    _name,
                                    type(exc).__name__,
                                    exc,
                                )
                                return {
                                    'status': 'error',
                                    'error_type': type(exc).__name__,
                                    'error': str(exc),
                                }

                        object.__setattr__(st, 'run_async', _wrapped_run_async)
                        object.__setattr__(st, '_swegemma_wrapped', True)

    sub_agents = getattr(agent, 'sub_agents', None)
    if isinstance(sub_agents, (list, tuple)):
        for sub in sub_agents:
            _wrap_skill_toolset_tools(sub, _visited)


def _extract_declared_tools(agent: Any) -> set[str] | None:
    """Extract the set of declared tool names across the compiled agent hierarchy.

    Returns None if the agent does not expose concrete tool lists (e.g., a mock object).
    """
    if agent is None:
        return None

    declared: set[str] = set()
    found_concrete_list = False
    visited: set[int] = set()

    def _walk(curr: Any) -> None:
        nonlocal found_concrete_list
        if curr is None:
            return
        curr_id = id(curr)
        if curr_id in visited:
            return
        visited.add(curr_id)

        raw_tools = getattr(curr, 'tools', None)
        if isinstance(raw_tools, (list, tuple)):
            found_concrete_list = True
            for t in raw_tools:
                if isinstance(t, str):
                    declared.add(t)
                    continue
                t_name = getattr(t, 'name', None) or getattr(t, '__name__', None)
                if isinstance(t_name, str) and t_name:
                    declared.add(t_name)
                inner_agent = getattr(t, 'agent', None)
                if inner_agent is not None:
                    _walk(inner_agent)
                inner_tools = getattr(t, '_tools', None)
                if isinstance(inner_tools, (list, tuple)):
                    for st in inner_tools:
                        st_name = getattr(st, 'name', None) or getattr(
                            st, '__name__', None
                        )
                        if isinstance(st_name, str) and st_name:
                            declared.add(st_name)

        sub_agents = getattr(curr, 'sub_agents', None)
        if isinstance(sub_agents, (list, tuple)):
            for sub in sub_agents:
                _walk(sub)

    _walk(agent)
    return declared if found_concrete_list else None


class ToolErrorPlugin(BasePlugin):
    """ADK plugin that converts undeclared-tool and runtime tool errors into tool responses."""

    def __init__(self, name: str = 'swegemma_tool_error') -> None:
        super().__init__(name=name)

    async def before_tool_callback(
        self,
        *,
        tool: Any,
        tool_args: dict[str, Any],
        tool_context: Any,
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """Coerce single-element list strings and validate skill tool arguments before execution."""
        tool_name = getattr(tool, 'name', '') or ''
        return _sanitize_skill_tool_args(tool_name, tool_args)

    async def on_tool_error_callback(
        self,
        *,
        tool: Any,
        tool_args: dict[str, Any],
        tool_context: Any,
        error: Exception,
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """Return tool lookup and runtime exceptions to the model as structured error dicts."""
        tool_name = getattr(tool, 'name', 'unknown') or 'unknown'
        logger.warning(
            "Tool '%s' raised %s: %s",
            tool_name,
            type(error).__name__,
            error,
        )
        return {
            'status': 'error',
            'error_type': type(error).__name__,
            'error': str(error),
        }


_PRUNED_TOP_DIRS = (
    'docs',
    'docs_src',
    'tests',
    'test_data',
    'examples',
    'benchmarks',
    'assets',
    'imgs',
    'questions',
    'site',
    'g3doc',
    'google3',
)


def build_workspace_tree_command(
    max_lines: int = 200,
    workspace_dir: str = '/workspace',
) -> str:
    """Build shell command to capture a breadth-first, pruned workspace layout tree."""
    prune_clauses = ' '.join(f'-not -path "./{d}/*"' for d in _PRUNED_TOP_DIRS)
    return (
        f'cd {workspace_dir} && find . -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 3 '
        '-not -path "./.*" -not -name ".*" '
        '-not -name "*.pyc" -not -name "__pycache__" -not -path "*/__pycache__/*" '
        '-not -path "./*.egg-info*" '
        f'{prune_clauses} '
        f"| awk -F/ '{{print NF, $0}}' | sort -n -k1,1 -k2 | head -{max_lines} "
        "| cut -d' ' -f2- | sort"
    )


def build_agent_prompt(
    task: Task | Any,
    config: EvalConfig | Any,
    workspace_tree: str = '',
    *,
    enable_sandbox_testing: bool | None = None,
    declared_tools: Iterable[str] | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Build the initial prompt for the software engineering agent."""
    if enable_sandbox_testing is None:
        cfg_testing = getattr(config, 'enable_sandbox_testing', None)
        enable_sandbox_testing = (
            bool(cfg_testing) if isinstance(cfg_testing, bool) else True
        )

    declared_set: set[str] | None = (
        set(declared_tools) if declared_tools is not None else None
    )

    def _has_tool(tool_name: str) -> bool:
        return declared_set is None or tool_name in declared_set

    budget_lines = []
    if getattr(config, 'budget', None):
        if config.budget.time_minutes is not None:
            budget_lines.append(
                f'- Time allowance: {config.budget.time_minutes} minutes'
            )
        if config.budget.tool_calls is not None:
            budget_lines.append(
